--- C:\games\steam\steamapps\workshop\content\108600\3811256204\mods\ProjectViewpointQOL\mod.info ---
name=Project Viewpoint QOL
id=ProjectViewpointQOL
description=Quality of life companion mod for Project Viewpoint in Build 42.
description=
description=Features:
description=- Auto mouse unlock when opening inventory (via Tab or UI)
description=- Auto mouse re-lock to camera look when inventory is closed (Tab / ESC / 'X')
description=- Auto mouse unlock on radial menus (Vehicle 'V', Emote 'Q', Firearm 'R', Light 'F')
description=- Auto mouse unlock on appliances, TVs, radios, World Map ('M'), crafting/building workstations, and health panel
description=- Cursor lock (Viewpoint's cursor key, middle mouse by default, or a Lock Cursor Hotkey) returns to camera look while keeping inventory/container panels open; optional Lock-to-Close also closes popups (off by default)
description=- Auto-uncollapse collapsed inventory on Tab
description=- Hotbar Cycling: hold the configured modifier (default Shift) and scroll the mouse wheel to cycle attached weapons/tools; works while sprinting (toggleable)
description=- In-world Container Equip: [G] prompt to equip weapons, clothing, and bags (backpacks, handbags, briefcases, ...), or pick where to put them (back, belt, holster, fanny pack front/back, hood up/down, off-hand for weapons, tools and bags, etc.)
description=- Loot Destination Hotkey: press [V] while aiming at a container or with the loot window open to choose where loot goes (inventory or an equipped bag); the current destination is shown in the loot hint
description=- Loot Menu Toggle: [K] to hide/show Viewpoint's crosshair loot menu (stops hover popups and stutter; syncs with Viewpoint's own option)
description=- Store All hotkey: hold the All Containers key (default [) to send your inventory into the open or aimed container (prompt shown in the crosshair hint; favorites/keys/equipped/hotbar protected; optional dedicated key can be bound)
description=- Loot All protection: optionally skip favorites and keys on Viewpoint Loot All and the loot window's Loot all (off by default)
description=- See All Nearby Containers: tap [ while looking at a container to show loot from every container within reach (tap again for the aimed one; hold to Store All)
description=- In-world Container Read: [G] prompt to read skill books and magazines directly from bookshelves/containers
description=- In-world Container Ingest: [G] to smoke/take pills instantly or open a portion chooser (Quarter/Half/All) for food and drinks directly from containers
description=- In-world Container Crafting: [B] on a recipe ingredient in a container or on the ground opens the Crafting window pre-filtered to that item (vanilla & Neat Crafting); opening it with no container item starts with a clear search
description=- Quick First-Aid: [G] prompt to bandage, disinfect, or suture wounds directly from world containers or personal inventory (shares the Item Action key)
description=- In-world Ammo Unpack & Load: [G] prompt to unpack ammo boxes/cartons and load magazines/guns directly (shares the Item Action key)
description=- Hands-Free Auto-Walk: ['] or modifier + forward (default Right Alt+W) to walk hands-free with first-person mouse steering
description=- Auto-Open Doors & Gates: jog or sprint into a closed, unlocked door or gate and it opens instantly without slowing you down (on by default; locked/barricaded doors still block)
description=- Fast-Forward While Auto-Walking: keeps your chosen fast-forward speed while auto-walking; manual movement and Shift/Sprint drop to normal like vanilla, or enable the optional manual mode to keep fast-forward while walking, running or turning (auto-slows near zombies with a tunable radius, both toggleable)
description=- Third-Person Strafing (optional): Viewpoint forces turn-to-move in third person; enable to honor Viewpoint's Strafe movement setting there (strafe and backpedal in 3rd person with the character turning with the camera, like in 1st); walks at normal speed by default (toggleable)
description=- Panini in first person only: keeps Viewpoint's Panini projection for first person and turns it off in third person
description=- Viewpoint Hands (Loot & Inventory): when active, loot/container, cursor, first-aid and wheel-camera features are handed over to Hands; classic camera, effects, Panini, strafing, auto-walk and speed control stay active. Both mods can be enabled together (Hands' incompatible entry for this mod is lifted at load).
description=- Panel Suppression: Seamlessly suppresses Character Health/Info panels, Crafting menu, and safety toggles during container actions
description=- Camera zoom: scroll in 3rd person to smoothly move the camera in/out by Viewpoint's own camera distance setting (fully zoomed in switches to 1st person), on foot and in vehicles; hold CTRL + scroll to adjust FOV in both views (both tunable in the QOL tab or Mod Options); 3D and top-down zoom stay independent (scrolling in 3D never changes the top-down zoom level)
description=- Classic camera toggle: removes Viewpoint 0.1.6's follow-camera float, turns off camera motion (sway) and automatic shoulder swap; restores your previous values when disabled
description=- Disable extra camera effects: removes Viewpoint's reaction camera moves (bite push-in and wobble, knockdown dip, death cam, climb/getup shifts) while leaving normal camera motion alone
description=- Configurable hotkeys and toggles in both Viewpoint's in-game settings window (DELETE -> QOL tab) and Build 42 Mod Options; the two stay in sync
description=- Multiplayer: client-side mod; works on servers that do not run it - keep ZombieBuddy, Viewpoint and this mod enabled on your PC and the client adds this mod (and its required mods) to the session's client mod list automatically (console.txt: [ProjectViewpointQOL] line). Servers can optionally enable all three (Mods=ZombieBuddy;Viewpoint;ProjectViewpointQOL; WorkshopItems=3619862853;3809306528;3811256204) so every player installs them automatically. Every player needs ZombieBuddy's one-time setup and must approve this mod's JAR on first launch (re-approve after mod updates); if QOL features stop working on a server, hold Shift while the game loads to force ZombieBuddy's approval dialog and check console.txt for [ProjectViewpointQOL] lines; if the server installs them, list order is ZombieBuddy, then Viewpoint, then this mod
description=- Load Order: load after ZombieBuddy and Viewpoint (declared automatically); the mod retries the Java bridge automatically and recovers from a wrong order without a restart
description=- On-screen notifications toggle: silence QOL popup messages (auto-walk, loot menu, zombie slowdown, moved keybinds, bridge warnings); the injury alert HUD keeps its own toggle
description=- Viewpoint's native cursor toggle (Mouse 3 by default) is preserved and followed when rebound
description=
description=Compatibility:
description=- Viewpoint Advanced Movement 1.3.3+: free look is Viewpoint's own (Mouse 4); our Auto-Walk uses Right Alt, hotbar cycling uses Shift+wheel; grab stays on F and other colliding Viewpoint keys move to a free key automatically; the All Containers key is [ so it never clashes with Viewpoint 0.1.6's shoulder swap (;)
description=- Neat Crafting: its inventory context searches ("Used in recipes" / "Created by recipes") are preserved when the crafting window opens
description=- Requires Viewpoint 0.1.6.1 exactly (pinned): on other versions the mod disables itself with a clear on-screen message until updated (Viewpoint asks addons to pin their version)
description=
description=Community & Support:
description=KodeMan's Creations Discord: https://discord.gg/6vMJSp76S3
description=Support the mod on Ko-fi: https://ko-fi.com/kodemann
description=
description=PZ ModGuard: A free pre-launch security scanner for Project Zomboid Build 42 mods (Windows, Linux & Steam Deck).
description=It inspects .jar and .class files, native files, launcher scripts and JVM agent arguments for malware that normal antivirus misses, with 3-tier threat detection, workshop-scoped whitelisting and SHA-256 exception pinning.
description=5 scan profiles, optional auto-scan before every Steam launch, zero dependencies. GitHub: https://github.com/KodeMannn/PZ-ModGuard
poster=poster.png
icon=icon.png
versionMin=42.21
require=\ZombieBuddy,\Viewpoint
loadModAfter=\ZombieBuddy,\Viewpoint
ZBVersionMin=2.3.0

description=- Auto-uncollapse collapsed inventory on Tab
description=- Hotbar Cycling: hold the configured modifier (default Shift) and scroll the mouse wheel to cycle attached weapons/tools; works while sprinting (toggleable)
description=- In-world Container Equip: [G] prompt to equip weapons, clothing, and bags (backpacks, handbags, briefcases, ...), or pick where to put them (back, belt, holster, fanny pack front/back, hood up/down, off-hand for weapons, tools and bags, etc.)
description=- Loot Destination Hotkey: press [V] while aiming at a container or with the loot window open to choose where loot goes (inventory or an equipped bag); the current destination is shown in the loot hint
description=- Loot Menu Toggle: [K] to hide/show Viewpoint's crosshair loot menu (stops hover popups and stutter; syncs with Viewpoint's own option)
description=- Store All hotkey: hold the All Containers key (default [) to send your inventory into the open or aimed container (prompt shown in the crosshair hint; favorites/keys/equipped/hotbar protected; optional dedicated key can be bound)
description=- Loot All protection: optionally skip favorites and keys on Viewpoint Loot All and the loot window's Loot all (off by default)
description=- See All Nearby Containers: tap [ while looking at a container to show loot from every container within reach (tap again for the aimed one; hold to Store All)
description=- In-world Container Read: [G] prompt to read skill books and magazines directly from bookshelves/containers
description=- In-world Container Ingest: [G] to smoke/take pills instantly or open a portion chooser (Quarter/Half/All) for food and drinks directly from containers
description=- In-world Container Crafting: [B] on a recipe ingredient in a container or on the ground opens the Crafting window pre-filtered to that item (vanilla & Neat Crafting); opening it with no container item starts with a clear search
description=- Quick First-Aid: [G] prompt to bandage, disinfect, or suture wounds directly from world containers or personal inventory (shares the Item Action key)
description=- In-world Ammo Unpack & Load: [G] prompt to unpack ammo boxes/cartons and load magazines/guns directly (shares the Item Action key)
description=- Hands-Free Auto-Walk: ['] or modifier + forward (default Right Alt+W) to walk hands-free with first-person mouse steering
description=- Auto-Open Doors & Gates: jog or sprint into a closed, unlocked door or gate and it opens instantly without slowing you down (on by default; locked/barricaded doors still block)
description=- Fast-Forward While Auto-Walking: keeps your chosen fast-forward speed while auto-walking; manual movement and Shift/Sprint drop to normal like vanilla, or enable the optional manual mode to keep fast-forward while walking, running or turning (auto-slows near zombies with a tunable radius, both toggleable)
description=- Third-Person Strafing (optional): Viewpoint forces turn-to-move in third person; enable to honor Viewpoint's Strafe movement setting there (strafe and backpedal in 3rd person with the character turning with the camera, like in 1st); walks at normal speed by default (toggleable)
description=- Panini in first person only: keeps Viewpoint's Panini projection for first person and turns it off in third person
description=- Viewpoint Hands (Loot & Inventory): when active, loot/container, cursor, first-aid and wheel-camera features are handed over to Hands; classic camera, effects, Panini, strafing, auto-walk and speed control stay active. Both mods can be enabled together (Hands' incompatible entry for this mod is lifted at load).
description=- Panel Suppression: Seamlessly suppresses Character Health/Info panels, Crafting menu, and safety toggles during container actions
description=- Camera zoom: scroll in 3rd person to smoothly move the camera in/out by Viewpoint's own camera distance setting (fully zoomed in switches to 1st person), on foot and in vehicles; hold CTRL + scroll to adjust FOV in both views (both tunable in the QOL tab or Mod Options); 3D and top-down zoom stay independent (scrolling in 3D never changes the top-down zoom level)
description=- Classic camera toggle: removes Viewpoint 0.1.6's follow-camera float, turns off camera motion (sway) and automatic shoulder swap; restores your previous values when disabled
description=- Disable extra camera effects: removes Viewpoint's reaction camera moves (bite push-in and wobble, knockdown dip, death cam, climb/getup shifts) while leaving normal camera motion alone
description=- Configurable hotkeys and toggles in both Viewpoint's in-game settings window (DELETE -> QOL tab) and Build 42 Mod Options; the two stay in sync
description=- Multiplayer: client-side mod; works on servers that do not run it - keep ZombieBuddy, Viewpoint and this mod enabled on your PC and the client adds this mod (and its required mods) to the session's client mod list automatically (console.txt: [ProjectViewpointQOL] line). Servers can optionally enable all three (Mods=ZombieBuddy;Viewpoint;ProjectViewpointQOL; WorkshopItems=3619862853;3809306528;3811256204) so every player installs them automatically. Every player needs ZombieBuddy's one-time setup and must approve this mod's JAR on first launch (re-approve after mod updates); if QOL features stop working on a server, hold Shift while the game loads to force ZombieBuddy's approval dialog and check console.txt for [ProjectViewpointQOL] lines; if the server installs them, list order is ZombieBuddy, then Viewpoint, then this mod
description=- Load Order: load after ZombieBuddy and Viewpoint (declared automatically); the mod retries the Java bridge automatically and recovers from a wrong order without a restart
description=- On-screen notifications toggle: silence QOL popup messages (auto-walk, loot menu, zombie slowdown, moved keybinds, bridge warnings); the injury alert HUD keeps its own toggle
description=- Viewpoint's native cursor toggle (Mouse 3 by default) is preserved and followed when rebound
description=
description=Compatibility:
description=- Viewpoint Advanced Movement 1.3.3+: free look is Viewpoint's own (Mouse 4); our Auto-Walk uses Right Alt, hotbar cycling uses Shift+wheel; grab stays on F and other colliding Viewpoint keys move to a free key automatically; the All Containers key is [ so it never clashes with Viewpoint 0.1.6's shoulder swap (;)
description=- Neat Crafting: its inventory context searches ("Used in recipes" / "Created by recipes") are preserved when the crafting window opens
description=- Requires Viewpoint 0.1.6.1 exactly (pinned): on other versions the mod disables itself with a clear on-screen message until updated (Viewpoint asks addons to pin their version)

WORKSHOP ID: 3811256204
--- C:\Games\Steam\steamapps\workshop\content\108600\3811256204\mods\ProjectViewpointQOL\mod.info ---
name=Project Viewpoint QOL
id=ProjectViewpointQOL
description=Quality of life companion mod for Project Viewpoint in Build 42.
description=
description=Features:
description=- Auto mouse unlock when opening inventory (via Tab or UI)
description=- Auto mouse re-lock to camera look when inventory is closed (Tab / ESC / 'X')
description=- Auto mouse unlock on radial menus (Vehicle 'V', Emote 'Q', Firearm 'R', Light 'F')
description=- Auto mouse unlock on appliances, TVs, radios, World Map ('M'), crafting/building workstations, and health panel
description=- Cursor lock (Viewpoint's cursor key, middle mouse by default, or a Lock Cursor Hotkey) returns to camera look while keeping inventory/container panels open; optional Lock-to-Close also closes popups (off by default)
description=- Auto-uncollapse collapsed inventory on Tab
description=- Hotbar Cycling: hold the configured modifier (default Shift) and scroll the mouse wheel to cycle attached weapons/tools; works while sprinting (toggleable)
description=- In-world Container Equip: [G] prompt to equip weapons, clothing, and bags (backpacks, handbags, briefcases, ...), or pick where to put them (back, belt, holster, fanny pack front/back, hood up/down, off-hand for weapons, tools and bags, etc.)
description=- Loot Destination Hotkey: press [V] while aiming at a container or with the loot window open to choose where loot goes (inventory or an equipped bag); the current destination is shown in the loot hint
description=- Loot Menu Toggle: [K] to hide/show Viewpoint's crosshair loot menu (stops hover popups and stutter; syncs with Viewpoint's own option)
description=- Store All hotkey: hold the All Containers key (default [) to send your inventory into the open or aimed container (prompt shown in the crosshair hint; favorites/keys/equipped/hotbar protected; optional dedicated key can be bound)
description=- Loot All protection: optionally skip favorites and keys on Viewpoint Loot All and the loot window's Loot all (off by default)
description=- See All Nearby Containers: tap [ while looking at a container to show loot from every container within reach (tap again for the aimed one; hold to Store All)
description=- In-world Container Read: [G] prompt to read skill books and magazines directly from bookshelves/containers
description=- In-world Container Ingest: [G] to smoke/take pills instantly or open a portion chooser (Quarter/Half/All) for food and drinks directly from containers
description=- In-world Container Crafting: [B] on a recipe ingredient in a container or on the ground opens the Crafting window pre-filtered to that item (vanilla & Neat Crafting); opening it with no container item starts with a clear search
description=- Quick First-Aid: [G] prompt to bandage, disinfect, or suture wounds directly from world containers or personal inventory (shares the Item Action key)
description=- In-world Ammo Unpack & Load: [G] prompt to unpack ammo boxes/cartons and load magazines/guns directly (shares the Item Action key)
description=- Hands-Free Auto-Walk: ['] or modifier + forward (default Right Alt+W) to walk hands-free with first-person mouse steering
description=- Auto-Open Doors & Gates: jog or sprint into a closed, unlocked door or gate and it opens instantly without slowing you down (on by default; locked/barricaded doors still block)
description=- Fast-Forward While Auto-Walking: keeps your chosen fast-forward speed while auto-walking; manual movement and Shift/Sprint drop to normal like vanilla, or enable the optional manual mode to keep fast-forward while walking, running or turning (auto-slows near zombies with a tunable radius, both toggleable)
description=- Third-Person Strafing (optional): Viewpoint forces turn-to-move in third person; enable to honor Viewpoint's Strafe movement setting there (strafe and backpedal in 3rd person with the character turning with the camera, like in 1st); walks at normal speed by default (toggleable)
description=- Panini in first person only: keeps Viewpoint's Panini projection for first person and turns it off in third person
description=- Viewpoint Hands (Loot & Inventory): when active, loot/container, cursor, first-aid and wheel-camera features are handed over to Hands; classic camera, effects, Panini, strafing, auto-walk and speed control stay active. Both mods can be enabled together (Hands' incompatible entry for this mod is lifted at load).
description=- Panel Suppression: Seamlessly suppresses Character Health/Info panels, Crafting menu, and safety toggles during container actions
description=- Camera zoom: scroll in 3rd person to smoothly move the camera in/out by Viewpoint's own camera distance setting (fully zoomed in switches to 1st person), on foot and in vehicles; hold CTRL + scroll to adjust FOV in both views (both tunable in the QOL tab or Mod Options); 3D and top-down zoom stay independent (scrolling in 3D never changes the top-down zoom level)
description=- Classic camera toggle: removes Viewpoint 0.1.6's follow-camera float, turns off camera motion (sway) and automatic shoulder swap; restores your previous values when disabled
description=- Disable extra camera effects: removes Viewpoint's reaction camera moves (bite push-in and wobble, knockdown dip, death cam, climb/getup shifts) while leaving normal camera motion alone
description=- Configurable hotkeys and toggles in both Viewpoint's in-game settings window (DELETE -> QOL tab) and Build 42 Mod Options; the two stay in sync
description=- Multiplayer: client-side mod; works on servers that do not run it - keep ZombieBuddy, Viewpoint and this mod enabled on your PC and the client adds this mod (and its required mods) to the session's client mod list automatically (console.txt: [ProjectViewpointQOL] line). Servers can optionally enable all three (Mods=ZombieBuddy;Viewpoint;ProjectViewpointQOL; WorkshopItems=3619862853;3809306528;3811256204) so every player installs them automatically. Every player needs ZombieBuddy's one-time setup and must approve this mod's JAR on first launch (re-approve after mod updates); if QOL features stop working on a server, hold Shift while the game loads to force ZombieBuddy's approval dialog and check console.txt for [ProjectViewpointQOL] lines; if the server installs them, list order is ZombieBuddy, then Viewpoint, then this mod
description=- Load Order: load after ZombieBuddy and Viewpoint (declared automatically); the mod retries the Java bridge automatically and recovers from a wrong order without a restart
description=- On-screen notifications toggle: silence QOL popup messages (auto-walk, loot menu, zombie slowdown, moved keybinds, bridge warnings); the injury alert HUD keeps its own toggle
description=- Viewpoint's native cursor toggle (Mouse 3 by default) is preserved and followed when rebound
description=
description=Compatibility:
description=- Viewpoint Advanced Movement 1.3.3+: free look is Viewpoint's own (Mouse 4); our Auto-Walk uses Right Alt, hotbar cycling uses Shift+wheel; grab stays on F and other colliding Viewpoint keys move to a free key automatically; the All Containers key is [ so it never clashes with Viewpoint 0.1.6's shoulder swap (;)
description=- Neat Crafting: its inventory context searches ("Used in recipes" / "Created by recipes") are preserved when the crafting window opens
description=- Requires Viewpoint 0.1.6.1 exactly (pinned): on other versions the mod disables itself with a clear on-screen message until updated (Viewpoint asks addons to pin their version)
description=
description=Community & Support:
description=KodeMan's Creations Discord: https://discord.gg/6vMJSp76S3
description=Support the mod on Ko-fi: https://ko-fi.com/kodemann
description=
description=PZ ModGuard: A free pre-launch security scanner for Project Zomboid Build 42 mods (Windows, Linux & Steam Deck).
description=It inspects .jar and .class files, native files, launcher scripts and JVM agent arguments for malware that normal antivirus misses, with 3-tier threat detection, workshop-scoped whitelisting and SHA-256 exception pinning.
description=5 scan profiles, optional auto-scan before every Steam launch, zero dependencies. GitHub: https://github.com/KodeMannn/PZ-ModGuard
poster=poster.png
icon=icon.png
versionMin=42.21
require=\ZombieBuddy,\Viewpoint
loadModAfter=\ZombieBuddy,\Viewpoint
ZBVersionMin=2.3.0
javaJarFile=media/java/client/ProjectViewpointQOL.jar
javaPkgName=viewpointqol

description= <LINE>
description=- Keep RMB available for aiming â€” enable the quick-click menu, or disable that shortcut entirely. Separate gun and throwable settings prevent it from opening while holding those items, leaving RMB available for aiming or another modâ€™s controls. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=- Adjust tap versus hold â€” choose how quickly RMB must be released to open the menu. Hold it longer to aim. <LINE>

description=- Rebind your controls â€” assign mouse buttons or keyboard keys for confirmation, rotation and cancellation. On-screen button hints follow your bindings. <LINE>

description=- Move and restyle hints â€” choose Project Zomboid, Project Viewpoint or NeatUI styling. Adjust horizontal and vertical position to fit your HUD, and hide button reminders when you no longer need them. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=- Adjust outlines â€” show or hide tile and wall outlines, change their thickness and toggle selected-object highlighting. <LINE>

description=<SIZE:medium> Frequently asked questions <SIZE:small> <LINE>
description=Where are the settings? How do I disable the right-click menu? <LINE>
description=Open Options > Mods > "Project Viewpoint - Isometric No More", and turn off "Open context menu on a quick RMB click". Building, furniture and item placement still work. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=How do I place items or move furniture? <LINE>
description=Use the inventory's "Place Item" action, or choose a mode from the game's furniture tool. Aim at the target, then click LMB to confirm. R rotates or cycles the selection; RMB cancels. The on-screen hints follow your bindings. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=Why does placement stop after one item? <LINE>
description=Turn off "Perform one action at a time" to keep the tool open. For inventory items selected together, hold Shift while confirming to place the stack. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=The menu interferes with aiming. What should I change? <LINE>
description=Enable "Disable menu with guns". It is on by default for new configurations; existing saved choices are kept. Throwables have a separate setting. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=Can I use the inventory while selecting a target? <LINE>
description=Use Viewpoint's "Cursor free or locked" binding (MMB by default). World confirmation pauses while the free pointer is over a window. RMB cancels the tool. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=How do I change furniture modes with a controller? <LINE>
description=Hold X, select a mode with the stick, then release X. This opens the game's furniture menu. Rebind "Furniture mode (hold)" in this mod's settings. Existing custom bindings take priority; assign this command if it is unbound. <LINE>
description= <LINE>
description=Nothing happens, or interactions stopped working. <LINE>
description=Check that both dependencies and the relevant feature module are enabled. Look at the status beside "Retry 3D interactions" in this mod's settings. Retry after a reported interaction error; fully restart after Java-component updates and approve the mod in ZombieBuddy if prompted. For a bug report, include console.txt, mod versions, the item name and the exact action used. <LINE>

description=Does this support every other mod or client-only multiplayer installation? <LINE>
description=Custom construction tools and placement systems may need their own integration. Dedicated-server and client-only setups have not been fully verified. Report your setup rather than assuming the same cause for every placement issue. <LINE>
